🧭 Reinforcement LearningReinforcement Learning

· 发展脉络· Development

强化学习（RL）在具身智能领域正经历从单一动作控制向多模态、长视界、高自由度全身协同的范式转变。早期研究聚焦于解决仿真到现实的迁移（Sim-to-Real）及接触式操作的稳定性问题，随后迅速演进至利用扩散模型、世界模型（World Models）及视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型来增强策略的泛化性与推理能力。当前趋势强调通过自进化、语义引导及混合架构（如分层RL与MPC结合）来实现复杂任务中的安全、高效与自适应控制，旨在构建具备通用物理认知与动态适应能力的机器人智能体。Reinforcement learning (RL) in embodied intelligence is undergoing a paradigm shift from single-action control to multimodal, long-horizon, high-degree-of-freedom whole-body coordination. Early research focused on solving Sim-to-Real transfer and stability in contact-based manipulation, then rapidly evolved to leveraging diffusion models, world models, and vision-language-action (VLA) models to enhance policy generalization and reasoning capabilities. Current trends emphasize achieving safe, efficient, and adaptive control in complex tasks through self-evolution, semantic guidance, and hybrid architectures (e.g., combining hierarchical RL with MPC), aiming to build robotic agents with general physical cognition and dynamic adaptability.

切入角度Perspectives

  • 扩散模型驱动的强化学习Diffusion Model-Driven Reinforcement Learning

  • 人机回环与混合策略Human-in-the-loop and hybrid strategies
  • 仿真到现实的物理鸿沟与触觉感知Sim-to-Real physical gap and tactile perception
  • 世界模型与幻觉治理World Models and Hallucination Governance
  • 安全与离线强化学习Safety and Offline Reinforcement Learning
  • 算法优化与训练技巧Algorithm Optimization and Training Techniques
  • 具身大模型与策略架构创新Embodied Large Models and Policy Architecture Innovation
  • 身体即计算与神经形态控制Body as Computation and Neuromorphic Control

· 各角度时间线· Timeline per Perspective

扩散模型驱动的强化学习Diffusion Model-Driven Reinforcement Learning

扩散模型驱动的强化学习旨在解决传统高斯策略无法建模多模态动作分布及在线训练样本效率低下的难题。该方向演进脉络清晰：早期尝试将扩散模型作为世界模型（如DIAMOND）或离线策略，随后通过Q加权变分优化（QVPO）和熵调节（DACER）将其引入在线RL，解决分布匹配与探索问题。中期聚焦于策略微调与推理加速，提出DPPO、RSM及噪声空间引导（Steering）等策略，平衡性能与延迟。近期研究转向价值扩散（Valdi）与最优传输加速（OTQL），试图在保持多模态优势的同时，克服计算瓶颈，推动从仿真到真机（如BeyondMimic）的落地应用。Diffusion model-driven reinforcement learning aims to address the challenges of traditional Gaussian policies failing to model multimodal action distributions and suffering from low online training sample efficiency. The evolution of this direction is clear: early attempts used diffusion models as world models (e.g., DIAMOND) or offline policies, subsequently introducing them into online RL via Q-weighted variational optimization (QVPO) and entropy regularization (DACER) to solve distribution matching and exploration problems. Mid-stage research focused on policy fine-tuning and inference acceleration, proposing strategies such as DPPO, RSM, and noise space steering to balance performance and latency. Recent studies have shifted towards value diffusion (Valdi) and optimal transport acceleration (OTQL), attempting to overcome computational bottlenecks while maintaining multimodal advantages, thereby promoting deployment from simulation to real-world applications (e.g., BeyondMimic).

2024-02
Foundation Policies with Hilbert Representations
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决传统离线强化学习中预训练目标缺乏统一几何视角的问题，试图利用希尔伯特空间的等距性质来捕捉状态间的时间距离，以增强零样本适应能力。Aiming to solve the lack of a unified geometric perspective in pretraining objectives for traditional offline RL, it attempts to utilize the isometric properties of Hilbert spaces to capture temporal distances between states, thereby enhancing zero-shot adaptability.
方法: Method: 提出HILP框架，将状态映射至保持时间距离的隐空间，通过简单的提示向量（prompt vector）引导策略覆盖所有方向，无需微调即可切换任务。Proposes the HILP framework, mapping states to a latent space that preserves temporal distances, guiding the policy to cover all directions via simple prompt vectors, enabling task switching without fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 在AntMaze和Kitchen等复杂任务上，证明了基于几何表示的离线预训练策略在零样本和Goal-conditioned设置下能超越专门设计的GCRL方法。Demonstrates on complex tasks like AntMaze and Kitchen that offline pretraining strategies based on geometric representations outperform specially designed GCRL methods under both zero-shot and Goal-conditioned settings.
2024-05
Diffusion for World Modeling: Visual Details Matter in Atari
动机: Motivation: 解决离散潜变量在构建世界模型时丢失视觉细节的问题，以生成高保真度的环境动态。Addressing the loss of visual details when constructing world models with discrete latent variables to generate high-fidelity environmental dynamics.
方法: Method: 利用扩散模型作为世界模型，在Atari环境中通过“想象”进行训练，替代传统的确定性潜变量模型。Utilizing diffusion models as world models to train via 'imagination' in Atari environments, replacing traditional deterministic latent variable models.
推进: Advance: 在Atari 100k基准上刷新世界模型代理纪录，证明了扩散模型在捕捉复杂视觉动态上的优越性，尽管推理成本较高。Breaking world model agent records on the Atari 100k benchmark, demonstrating the superiority of diffusion models in capturing complex visual dynamics, despite high inference costs.
2024-05
Diffusion-based Reinforcement Learning via Q-weighted Variational Policy Optimization
动机: Motivation: 克服扩散模型在在线强化学习中因缺乏“好样本”而难以直接训练目标分布的困境。Overcoming the dilemma in online reinforcement learning where diffusion models struggle to directly train target distributions due to the lack of 'good samples'.
方法: Method: 提出Q-weighted Variational Policy Optimization (QVPO)，利用Q值作为权重构建变分下界损失函数。Proposing Q-weighted Variational Policy Optimization (QVPO), which utilizes Q-values as weights to construct a variational lower bound loss function.
推进: Advance: 在MuJoCo上实现了扩散策略的在线训练，证明了理论上的紧下界在工程上的有效性，但受限于Q值准确性与推理延迟。Achieving online training of diffusion policies on MuJoCo, proving the engineering effectiveness of the theoretically tight lower bound, yet limited by Q-value accuracy and inference latency.
2024-05
Diffusion Actor-Critic with Entropy Regulator
动机: Motivation: 解决高斯策略无法处理多模态动作分布的问题，同时尝试在在线RL中实现最大熵目标。Addressing the inability of Gaussian policies to handle multimodal action distributions while attempting to achieve maximum entropy objectives in online RL.
方法: Method: 构建Diffusion Actor-Critic with Entropy Regulator (DACER)，通过高斯混合模型估算熵并调节反向扩散过程的噪声系数。Constructing Diffusion Actor-Critic with Entropy Regulator (DACER), which estimates entropy via Gaussian mixture models and regulates the noise coefficient of the reverse diffusion process.
推进: Advance: 在MuJoCo上证明了扩散策略的表示能力优于高斯策略，但计算开销大，属于以算力换取性能的非实时方案。Demonstrating on MuJoCo that diffusion policies possess superior representational capabilities compared to Gaussian policies, but with high computational overhead, constituting a non-real-time solution that trades compute for performance.
2024-09
Diffusion Policy Policy Optimization
动机: Motivation: 打破业界认为策略梯度（PG）方法难以有效微调扩散策略的固有认知，解决长视界任务的不稳定性。Breaking the industry consensus that policy gradient (PG) methods are ineffective for fine-tuning diffusion policies, resolving instability in long-horizon tasks.
方法: Method: 提出Diffusion Policy Policy Optimization (DPPO)，直接对预训练扩散策略进行策略梯度微调。Proposing Diffusion Policy Policy Optimization (DPPO), which directly fine-tunes pre-trained diffusion policies via policy gradients.
推进: Advance: 在长视界任务中展现出比Off-policy Q-learning更稳健的微调性能，确立了扩散策略在适应新环境时的实用价值。Demonstrating more robust fine-tuning performance than off-policy Q-learning in long-horizon tasks, establishing the practical value of diffusion policies in adapting to new environments.
2025-01
Efficient Online Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Policy
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略在在线强化学习中因采样效率低和训练不稳定导致的“水土不服”问题，旨在打破扩散模型必须严格采样自目标分布的固有约束。Aiming to address the poor adaptation issues caused by low sampling efficiency and unstable training of diffusion policies in online reinforcement learning, this work seeks to break the inherent constraint that diffusion models must strictly sample from the target distribution.
方法: Method: 提出重加权分数匹配（RSM）算法，通过解耦采样过程与目标分布的强制匹配，结合批量采样与Logsumexp技巧优化训练稳定性。Proposes the Reweighted Score Matching (RSM) algorithm, which decouples the sampling process from the forced matching of the target distribution, combining batch sampling with the Logsumexp trick to optimize training stability.
推进: Advance: 在MuJoCo高维连续控制任务中显著超越SAC等基线算法（提升超120%），证明了扩散策略在在线RL中具备理论可行性和性能优势，为多模态动作建模提供了新范式。Significantly outperforms baseline algorithms such as SAC in MuJoCo high-dimensional continuous control tasks (improvement of over 120%), proving the theoretical feasibility and performance advantages of diffusion policies in online RL, and providing a new paradigm for multimodal action modeling.
2025-06
Steering Your Diffusion Policy with Latent Space Reinforcement Learning
动机: Motivation: 避免直接微调扩散模型权重带来的梯度爆炸与数值不稳定问题，实现闭源模型的轻量级适配。Avoiding gradient explosion and numerical instability caused by directly fine-tuning diffusion model weights, enabling lightweight adaptation of closed-source models.
方法: Method: 提出Latent Space Reinforcement Learning，将噪声空间作为动作空间，通过RL直接“调教”输入噪声。Proposing Latent Space Reinforcement Learning, treating the noise space as the action space and using RL to directly 'train' the input noise.
推进: Advance: 实现了对基座扩散策略的黑盒访问与高效微调，在无需修改权重的情况下显著提升了模型对特定任务的适应性。Achieving black-box access and efficient fine-tuning of foundation diffusion policies, significantly enhancing model adaptability to specific tasks without modifying weights.
2025-08
BeyondMimic: From Motion Tracking to Versatile Humanoid Control via Guided Diffusion
动机: Motivation: 解决人形机器人动作单一、缺乏组合能力的问题，实现从动作追踪到通用控制的跨越。Addressing the issue of single-mode actions and lack of compositional capabilities in humanoid robots, achieving a leap from action tracking to general control.
方法: Method: 提出BeyondMimic，结合RL预训练与扩散模型的在线规划，利用引导扩散生成零样本组合技能。Proposing BeyondMimic, which combines RL pre-training with online planning in diffusion models to generate zero-shot compositional skills via guided diffusion.
推进: Advance: 在真机上实现了后空翻等复杂动作组合，证明了扩散模型在技能泛化上的潜力，但受限于状态估计精度与推理延迟。Achieving complex action combinations such as backflips on real robots, demonstrating the potential of diffusion models in skill generalization, yet limited by state estimation accuracy and inference latency.
2026-07
Valdi: Value Diffusion World Models
动机: Motivation: 尝试将扩散模型的“想象力”融入实时规划循环，替代传统确定性价值模型以增强多模态预测。Attempting to integrate the 'imagination' of diffusion models into real-time planning loops, replacing traditional deterministic value models to enhance multimodal prediction.
方法: Method: 提出Valdi，使用单步扩散替代TD-MPC中的确定性MLP，构建价值引导的扩散世界模型。Proposing Valdi, which uses single-step diffusion to replace deterministic MLPs in TD-MPC, constructing a value-guided diffusion world model.
推进: Advance: 证明了Latent Diffusion在在线控制中的可行性，但也揭示了在价值引导循环中扩散步数增加可能导致性能下降的悖论。Demonstrating the feasibility of Latent Diffusion in online control, while also revealing the paradox that increasing diffusion steps in a value-guided loop may lead to performance degradation.
2026-07
Optimal Transport Q-Learning for Flow Policy Steering and Acceleration
动机: Motivation: 解决扩散/流策略推理速度慢及微调困难的问题，通过最优传输加速策略更新。Addressing the slow inference speed and difficulty in fine-tuning diffusion/flow policies by accelerating policy updates via optimal transport.
方法: Method: 提出Optimal Transport Q-Learning (OTQL)，利用神经ODE与最优传输损失对策略进行加速与蒸馏。Proposing Optimal Transport Q-Learning (OTQL), which accelerates and distills policies using neural ODEs and optimal transport losses.
推进: Advance: 在极少量交互下显著提升成功率并减少70%推理步数，为VLA微调提供了高效的加速方案，但依赖环境重置与ODE结构。Significantly improving success rates and reducing inference steps by 70% with minimal interaction, providing an efficient acceleration scheme for VLA fine-tuning, yet dependent on environment resets and ODE structures.
2026-07
Reinforcement Learning: From Algorithms To Foundation Models
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决传统高斯策略无法建模多模态动作分布的难题，并试图利用扩散模型为强化学习注入通用的世界知识。Aiming to solve the challenge that traditional Gaussian policies cannot model multi-modal action distributions, and attempting to inject general world knowledge into reinforcement learning using diffusion models.
方法: Method: 构建了FightLadder格斗游戏平台，提出Diffusion World Model作为环境模拟器，并探索将Consistency Model作为策略以平衡推理速度与生成质量。Constructed the FightLadder fighting game platform, proposed Diffusion World Model as an environment simulator, and explored using Consistency Model as a policy to balance inference speed and generation quality.
推进: Advance: 验证了扩散模型在复杂博弈环境中构建多模态策略的潜力，但揭示了视频生成与RL结合时存在的落地延迟与显存开销挑战。Verified the potential of diffusion models in constructing multi-modal policies in complex gaming environments, but revealed challenges regarding deployment latency and memory overhead when combining video generation with RL.
2026-07
X-NavDP: Generalizing Navigation Diffusion Policy to Novel Behavior and Embodiments with Group Q-score Reweighted Matching
动机: Motivation: 针对预训练扩散导航策略（如NavDP）在面对死胡同或新机器人形态时泛化能力不足的问题，旨在利用强化学习弥补模仿学习在复杂动态环境中的盲区。Aiming to address the insufficient generalization capability of pre-trained diffusion navigation policies (such as NavDP) when facing dead ends or new robot morphologies, this work seeks to leverage reinforcement learning to fill the blind spots of imitation learning in complex dynamic environments.
方法: Method: 提出GQRM框架，将强化学习作为后训练手段对扩散策略进行微调，通过引入RL信号优化策略在未知行为和新具身形态下的决策能力。Proposing the GQRM framework, which utilizes reinforcement learning as a post-training method to fine-tune diffusion policies, optimizing decision-making capabilities under unknown behaviors and new embodied morphologies by introducing RL signals.
推进: Advance: 成功将仿真环境下的导航成功率从61%提升至84%，真机表现从10%跃升至65%，证明了RL微调能有效赋予扩散策略“绕路”等复杂导航能力。Successfully improving navigation success rates from 61% to 84% in simulation environments and from 10% to 65% in real-world deployments, demonstrating that RL fine-tuning can effectively endow diffusion policies with complex navigation capabilities such as 'detouring'.
2026-07
A Single Diffusion-Policy Controller for Multi-Task Block Pushing with Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略通常依赖行为克隆（BC）且难以在接触丰富任务中实现零仿真到真实迁移的痛点，试图探索仅凭稀疏奖励从头在线训练扩散策略的可行性。Addressing the pain point that diffusion policies typically rely on Behavior Cloning (BC) and struggle with zero-simulation-to-real transfer in contact-rich tasks, it explores the feasibility of training diffusion policies from scratch online using only sparse rewards.
方法: Method: 设计ESM损失函数简化RL与扩散模型的结合，在无需专家演示的情况下，通过在线强化学习直接优化多任务推块策略。Designs the ESM loss function to simplify the combination of RL and diffusion models, directly optimizing multi-task push-block policies via online RL without requiring expert demonstrations.
推进: Advance: 成功实现了多任务推块场景下的零仿真到真实迁移，证明了扩散策略在接触丰富任务中比SAC等经典算法更具鲁棒性，且无需依赖大规模演示数据。Successfully achieves zero-simulation-to-real transfer in multi-task push-block scenarios, proving that diffusion policies are more robust than classic algorithms like SAC in contact-rich tasks and do not rely on large-scale demonstration data.
2026-08
Diffusion Policy with Behavioral Advantage Correction for Offline Reinforcement Learning
动机: Motivation: 致力于解决离线强化学习中保守策略（如CQL）因过度防过估计而导致的“保守病”，即错误地丢弃数据稀疏但具有高潜力的动作分布。Dedicated to solving the 'conservative disease' in offline RL conservative policies (e.g., CQL), where excessive fear of overestimation leads to incorrectly discarding action distributions that are data-sparse but have high potential.
方法: Method: 提出DPBAC算法，利用行为策略的Q值作为自适应标尺来修正扩散策略的评估偏差，在保持多模态建模能力的同时平衡过估计与欠估计风险。Proposes the DPBAC algorithm, utilizing Q-values of the behavior policy as an adaptive scale to correct evaluation bias in diffusion policies, balancing overestimation and underestimation risks while maintaining multimodal modeling capabilities.
推进: Advance: 在D4RL基准上实现了优异性能，证明了该方法能有效克服传统保守策略的局限性，为扩散模型在离线RL中的理论严谨性与工程实用性结合提供了新路径。Achieves excellent performance on the D4RL benchmark, proving that this method effectively overcomes the limitations of traditional conservative policies and provides a new path for combining theoretical rigor with engineering practicality for diffusion models in offline RL.
2026-08
Learning When to Stop: Prefix-Optimal Dynamic Diffusion Policies for Continuous Control
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略固定推理步数导致计算冗余及无法根据环境状态动态调整决策深度的问题，旨在打破传统扩散模型“死板”执行固定步数的限制。Aiming to break the limitation of traditional diffusion models rigidly executing a fixed number of steps, which causes computational redundancy and inability to dynamically adjust decision depth based on environmental states in diffusion policies.
方法: Method: 提出前缀最优动态扩散策略（POGP），利用Bellman递归将价值函数反向传播至去噪过程的中间步骤，将中间状态视为有效动作并引入动态停止机制。Proposes Prefix Optimal Dynamic Diffusion Policy (POGP), utilizing Bellman recursion to backpropagate value functions to intermediate steps of the denoising process, treating intermediate states as valid actions and introducing a dynamic stopping mechanism.
推进: Advance: 实现了在保持策略质量的同时将推理速度提升2.7倍，证明了动态调整扩散步数在连续控制任务中的可行性，但牺牲了部分多模态生成能力以换取确定性。Achieved a 2.7x increase in inference speed while maintaining policy quality, demonstrating the feasibility of dynamically adjusting diffusion steps in continuous control tasks, but at the cost of sacrificing some multi-modal generation capabilities for determinism.
2026-08
PFM-HR: Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots
动机: Motivation: 为解决传统时序先验依赖有序片段且忽略关节协同几何约束的问题，试图利用无序姿态数据构建通用的流匹配（Flow Matching）先验以指导具身智能。Aiming to utilize unordered pose data to construct a general Flow Matching prior to guide embodied intelligence, addressing the issue that traditional temporal priors rely on ordered segments and ignore joint coordination geometric constraints.
方法: Method: 构建姿态流匹配（PFM-HR）框架，引入去噪器Jacobian评估的几何一致性评分（PGS）机制，利用无序姿态数据训练可复用的先验模型。Constructs the Pose Flow Matching (PFM-HR) framework, introducing a Geometric Consistency Score (PGS) mechanism evaluated by denoiser Jacobians, utilizing unordered pose data to train reusable prior models.
推进: Advance: 在动态动作生成上优于现有方法，成功将静态几何约束引入动态过程指导，但本质上仍依赖RL探索时序逻辑，未能完全解决动作时序连贯性的硬伤。Outperforms existing methods in dynamic action generation and successfully introduces static geometric constraints into dynamic process guidance, but essentially still relies on RL exploration for temporal logic, failing to fully resolve the hard flaw of action temporal coherence.
2026-08
Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决水下多智能体在动态拓扑和弱通信环境下，传统强化学习难以有效协同追踪目标的痛点。Aiming to address the pain point that traditional reinforcement learning struggles to effectively coordinate multi-agent target tracking in dynamic topologies and weak communication environments underwater.
方法: Method: 提出价值梯度引导机制，将扩散去噪过程显式地拉向高回报区域，以替代纯随机采样。Proposing a value gradient-guided mechanism that explicitly pulls the diffusion denoising process toward high-reward regions to replace purely random sampling.
推进: Advance: 验证了将价值函数引入扩散采样的可行性，显著提升了收敛速度与轨迹稳定性，为复杂通信约束下的多模态策略提供了新思路。Validating the feasibility of incorporating value functions into diffusion sampling, significantly improving convergence speed and trajectory stability, and providing new insights for multi-modal strategies under complex communication constraints.
2026-08
RoMAN-Flow: Taming Autoregressive Normalizing Flows for Offline Reinforcement Learning in Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对自回归归一化流（AR-NF）在离线强化学习中采样效率低下的问题，旨在利用其精确似然优势同时解决推理延迟灾难。Aiming to address the low sampling efficiency of autoregressive normalizing flows (AR-NF) in offline reinforcement learning, this work seeks to leverage their exact likelihood advantages while resolving the inference latency catastrophe.
方法: Method: 提出NF-IQL避免训练时采样，并采用蒸馏技术将慢速的AR-NF压缩为一步生成模型以加速推理。Proposing NF-IQL to avoid sampling during training, and employing distillation techniques to compress the slow AR-NF into a one-step generation model to accelerate inference.
推进: Advance: 证明了AR-NF在离线RL中的可行性，在保持精确似然的同时实现了推理速度的显著提升，填补了生成模型在实时控制中的空白。Demonstrating the feasibility of AR-NF in offline RL, achieving a significant improvement in inference speed while maintaining exact likelihood, thereby filling the gap in real-time control for generative models.
2026-08
ADAPT: Physics-Aware Diffusion-based World Models for Adaptive Predictive Transferable HVAC Control
动机: Motivation: 解决纯数据驱动的扩散世界模型在跨季节、跨气候等分布外（OOD）场景下泛化能力差及能耗优化不足的问题。Addressing the poor generalization capability and insufficient energy optimization of purely data-driven diffusion world models in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios such as cross-season and cross-climate conditions.

iADD: Improving Alignment and Diversity in Diffusion Policy Optimization
动机: Motivation: 针对现有扩散策略优化方法（如DDPO）在通过强化学习对齐奖励时，往往牺牲动作分布多样性导致生成内容单一或失真的问题。Addressing the issue where existing diffusion policy optimization methods (such as DDPO) often sacrifice action distribution diversity when aligning rewards via reinforcement learning, leading to generated content that is either singular or distorted.
方法: Method: 提出基于Feynman-Kac路径引导的优化框架，并证明在扩散过程早期时间步进行策略更新比仅更新后期步更能有效维持多样性。Proposing an optimization framework based on Feynman-Kac path guidance and proving that updating policies at early time steps of the diffusion process is more effective than updating only at later steps in maintaining diversity.
推进: Advance: 从理论上修正了“仅更新后期时间步”的流行观点，在保持奖励对齐的同时显著提升了生成策略的多样性，解决了传统方法“为了对齐不要脸”的缺陷。Theoretically correcting the popular view of 'updating only at later time steps', significantly enhancing the diversity of generated policies while maintaining reward alignment, thereby resolving the defect of traditional methods that 'sacrifice all for alignment'.

Reachability-Aware Diffusion Policy Optimization
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略在复杂环境中缺乏显式安全约束、易导致高风险探索的问题，旨在解决传统方法仅在事后惩罚而缺乏事前预警的缺陷。Aiming to address the lack of explicit safety constraints in diffusion policies within complex environments, which often leads to high-risk exploration, and to overcome the defect of traditional methods that only apply post-hoc penalties without pre-emptive warnings.
方法: Method: 提出RADPO框架，利用可达性预测（Reachability Prediction）计算首次命中时间作为风险信号，并通过预算反馈机制动态调节惩罚力度以引导策略避开危险区域。Proposing the RADPO framework, which utilizes Reachability Prediction to calculate the time-to-first-hit as a risk signal, and dynamically adjusts penalty intensity through a budget feedback mechanism to guide policies away from hazardous regions.
推进: Advance: 将安全机制从被动累积成本惩罚转变为基于“首次命中”的主动风险规避，实现了在模型无关设置下对扩散策略的在线安全优化，但受限于离线数据上的幸存者偏差，尚未提供严格的约束保证。Transforming safety mechanisms from passive cumulative cost penalties to active risk avoidance based on 'first-hit' events, achieving online safety optimization for diffusion policies in model-agnostic settings, yet limited by survivorship bias in offline data and lacking strict constraint guarantees.

Factorized Tactile Representation and Control for Sim-to-Real Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对传统扩散策略在触觉控制中因信号耦合导致的多模态分布建模困难及Sim-to-Real迁移效率低下的问题，旨在解决复杂触觉信号难以统一表征的痛点。Aiming to address the difficulties in modeling multimodal distributions and the low efficiency of Sim-to-Real transfer in traditional diffusion policies for tactile control caused by signal coupling, this work targets the pain point of unified representation of complex tactile signals.
方法: Method: 提出因子化触觉表示框架，将接触响应解耦为几何、力和时序变化三个独立因子，并设计Tactile Gated Policy实现推理时的动态输入切换。Proposing a factorized tactile representation framework that decouples contact responses into three independent factors: geometry, force, and temporal variation, and designing a Tactile Gated Policy to enable dynamic input switching during inference.
推进: Advance: 通过因子化解耦有效提升了触觉信息的利用效率，实现了无需重新训练即可适应不同任务依赖的动态策略，为扩散模型驱动的多模态触觉控制提供了新的迁移范式。Effectively improving the utilization efficiency of tactile information through factorized decoupling, achieving dynamic policies that adapt to different task dependencies without retraining, and providing a new transfer paradigm for diffusion model-driven multimodal tactile control.

具身大模型 (VLA) 的强化学习微调Embodied Large Model (VLA) Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning

该方向旨在解决VLA模型从静态模仿学习向动态自主决策演进中的核心瓶颈。早期工作（如#9, #10）尝试引入RL以增强推理与规划，但面临训练成本高昂、仿真与真实世界差距大及奖励稀疏等挑战。演进路径逐渐从简单的PPO微调转向更高效的策略（如#34的GRPO、#44的价值校准），并聚焦于解决CoT的“废话文学”问题（#30）及提升奖励信号的可靠性（#62, #72）。近期研究（#29, #53）开始探索无需外部奖励的直觉驱动及测试时优化，试图在降低计算成本的同时，赋予模型真正的长程规划与泛化能力，推动VLA从“听话”走向“会思考”。This direction aims to address the core bottleneck in the evolution of VLA models from static imitation learning to dynamic autonomous decision-making. Early works (e.g., #9, #10) attempted to introduce RL to enhance reasoning and planning but faced challenges such as high training costs, the sim-to-real gap, and sparse rewards. The evolutionary path gradually shifted from simple PPO fine-tuning to more efficient strategies (e.g., GRPO in #34, value calibration in #44), focusing on solving the 'nonsense literature' problem of CoT (#30) and improving the reliability of reward signals (#62, #72). Recent studies (#29, #53) have begun exploring intuition-driven approaches and test-time optimization without external rewards, attempting to reduce computational costs while endowing models with true long-horizon planning and generalization capabilities, pushing VLA from 'obedient' to 'capable of thinking'.

MARS-RA: Rank Aggregation for Credit Assignment via Multimodal Comparisons in Embodied Multi-Agent Cooperation
动机: Motivation: 针对具身大模型强化学习中传统信用分配方法难以量化多模态贡献且依赖人工标注的痛点，试图利用大模型的视觉理解能力替代人工裁判。Addressing the pain point that traditional credit allocation methods in embodied large model reinforcement learning struggle to quantify multi-modal contributions and rely on manual annotation, this work attempts to utilize the visual understanding capabilities of large models to replace human judges.
方法: Method: 提出MARS-RA框架，将信用分配问题重构为基于多模态比较的排名聚合任务，利用大语言模型作为裁判对策略表现进行相对排序而非绝对打分。Proposing the MARS-RA framework, which reframes the credit allocation problem as a rank aggregation task based on multi-modal comparison, utilizing large language models as judges to perform relative ranking of policy performance rather than absolute scoring.
推进: Advance: 通过引入Shapley值理论作为解释器，在无需外部奖励信号的情况下实现了比QMIX更优的信用分配效果，验证了利用大模型幻觉进行相对评估的可行性，但揭示了其对模型视觉鲁棒性的高度依赖。By introducing Shapley value theory as an explainer, achieving superior credit allocation effects compared to QMIX without external reward signals, validating the feasibility of relative evaluation using large model hallucinations, but revealing a high dependency on the model's visual robustness.

ExToken: Structured Exploration for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Fine-tuning
动机: Motivation: 针对VLA-RL中因标准随机采样导致的“动作模式坍塌”与探索停滞问题，试图通过结构化探索提升策略多样性。Addressing the 'action mode collapse' and exploration stagnation caused by standard random sampling in VLA-RL, it attempts to enhance policy diversity through structured exploration.
方法: Method: 利用离线演示数据聚类生成行为Token（ExToken），强制策略在不同Token模式间进行探索，而非单纯增加采样量。Utilizes offline demonstration data clustering to generate Behavior Tokens (ExToken), forcing the policy to explore across different Token modes rather than simply increasing sampling volume.
推进: Advance: 揭示了Token数量K对探索稳定性的敏感性，证明了结构化行为Token比单纯增加数据量更能有效打破局部最优，为低预算下的探索提供了新思路。Reveals the sensitivity of exploration stability to the number of Tokens K, proving that structured behavior tokens are more effective than simply increasing data volume in breaking local optima, providing new ideas for exploration under low budgets.

RedFlow: Redirect Failure into Action-Level Corrections for Flow-matching VLA Policy
动机: Motivation: 针对Flow-matching VLA在离线调优中浪费失败样本的问题，试图利用失败轨迹提升策略的修正能力与样本效率。Addressing the issue of Flow-matching VLA wasting failure samples during offline tuning, it attempts to utilize failure trajectories to enhance the policy's correction capability and sample efficiency.
方法: Method: 提出RedFlow框架，通过上下文匹配将失败动作映射为“替身”，利用成功动作进行修正，无需在线交互即可实现离线RL。Proposes the RedFlow framework, mapping failure actions to 'proxies' via context matching and using successful actions for correction, achieving offline RL without online interaction.
推进: Advance: 实现了比PPO等在线方法更高的样本效率，并在真实实验中展现了基线模型不具备的“失败恢复”行为（如拉回掉落衣物），但受限于预训练进度估计的准确性。Achieves higher sample efficiency than online methods like PPO and demonstrates 'failure recovery' behaviors (e.g., pulling back dropped clothes) in real experiments that baseline models lack, but is limited by the accuracy of pretraining progress estimation.

FAR: Failure-Aware Retry for Test-Time Recovery and Continual Policy Improvement
动机: Motivation: 解决机器人测试时遭遇失败后依赖人工干预或简单复读机式重试的问题，旨在实现无需人类介入的自主恢复与持续改进。Solves the problem of robots relying on human intervention or simple repetitive-style retries after encountering failures during testing, aiming to achieve autonomous recovery and continuous improvement without human intervention.
方法: Method: 提出FAR（Failure-Aware Retry），结合测试时微调与价值引导的偏好优化，利用失败数据自我修复策略。Proposes FAR (Failure-Aware Retry), combining test-time fine-tuning with value-guided preference optimization to self-repair policies using failure data.
推进: Advance: 证明了在Diffusion Policy上利用价值引导可实现自主恢复，但揭示了该方法对Critic准确性的强依赖，且在长程任务中仍可能面临视觉理解失效的风险。Proves that value guidance can achieve autonomous recovery on Diffusion Policy, but reveals a strong dependence on Critic accuracy and the potential risk of visual understanding failure in long-horizon tasks.

动机: Motivation: 针对VLA在状态良好时进行RL微调反而导致性能下降的“过拟合”现象，试图实现按需的自适应RL微调。Addressing the 'overfitting' phenomenon where RL fine-tuning on VLA leads to performance degradation when states are good, it attempts to achieve on-demand adaptive RL fine-tuning.
方法: Method: 提出RL²框架，在隐空间引入轻量级RL策略作为“开关”，仅在检测到状态恶化（SAFE检测器触发）时进行Latent Compositional Steering。Proposes the RL² framework, introducing a lightweight RL policy as a 'switch' in the latent space, performing Latent Compositional Steering only when state deterioration is detected (SAFE detector triggered).
推进: Advance: 发现了VLA的“考试式”Scaling Law，即仅在必要时介入RL可显著提升OOD泛化能力（提升17%），但引入了检测器失效导致框架崩溃的潜在风险。Discovers a 'exam-style' Scaling Law for VLA, where intervening with RL only when necessary significantly improves OOD generalization (by 17%), but introduces the potential risk of framework collapse due to detector failure.

动机: Motivation: 解决标准GRPO算法在机器人RL中因全成功或全失败样本组被丢弃而导致的早期训练样本浪费及策略收敛缓慢问题。Solving the problem of early training sample waste and slow policy convergence in robot RL caused by the standard GRPO algorithm discarding entire groups of samples that are either fully successful or fully failed.
方法: Method: 提出Prism-GRPO，通过基于轨迹质量对同结果组进行细分，保留部分失败样本的梯度信息，使模型能从“次优”轨迹中学习而非仅依赖完美样本。Proposing Prism-GRPO, which subdivides groups with the same outcome based on trajectory quality, retaining gradient information from some failed samples so the model can learn from 'suboptimal' trajectories rather than relying solely on perfect samples.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了样本效率，打破了传统RL在稀疏奖励下难以利用部分成功经验的僵局，为VLA在复杂物理交互中的高效微调提供了新的优化范式。Significantly improving sample efficiency and breaking the stalemate of traditional RL in utilizing partial success experiences under sparse rewards, providing a new optimization paradigm for efficient fine-tuning of VLA in complex physical interactions.

动机: Motivation: 解决视觉强化学习（Visual RL）中通用的数据增强策略在 VLA 后训练阶段导致 Actor-Critic 训练崩塌的痛点，特别是视觉鲁棒性不足的问题。Addressing the pain point of training collapse in Actor-Critic learning during the VLA post-training phase caused by generic data augmentation strategies in Visual RL, particularly regarding insufficient visual robustness.

Q-Learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching
动机: Motivation: 针对Flow Policy在RL微调中因逐层反向传播导致的计算资源消耗过高问题，旨在通过简化矩阵运算来降低训练门槛。Aiming to reduce training barriers by simplifying matrix operations to address the excessive computational resource consumption caused by layer-by-layer backpropagation in RL fine-tuning of Flow Policy.
方法: Method: 利用预训练Flow策略雅可比矩阵的对角线特性，推导闭式标量伴随（Scalar Adjoint）替代昂贵的向量-雅可比积，并引入价值惩罚机制辅助策略生成。Leveraging the diagonal characteristics of the pre-trained Flow policy Jacobian matrix to derive a closed-form scalar adjoint to replace expensive vector-Jacobian products, and introducing a value penalty mechanism to assist policy generation.
推进: Advance: 成功将复杂的矩阵运算简化为标量缩放，在OGBench复杂任务上实现18-35%的性能提升并验证了真机微调的可行性，证明了工程直觉在特定近似下的有效性。Successfully simplifying complex matrix operations to scalar scaling, achieving a 18-35% performance improvement on the OGBench complex tasks and verifying the feasibility of real-robot fine-tuning, demonstrating the effectiveness of engineering intuition under specific approximations.
2026-10
Many Ways to Succeed: Diversity-Driven RL Fine-Tuning for VLA Generalization
动机: Motivation: 为了解决VLA在RL微调中因策略趋同导致的“走捷径”现象及局部最优内卷，旨在通过显式奖励多样性来打破泛化瓶颈。Aiming to break generalization bottlenecks by explicitly rewarding diversity to address the 'shortcutting' phenomenon and local optimization involution in VLA RL fine-tuning caused by policy convergence.
方法: Method: 提出DRIVE框架，利用高斯核函数（GAK）计算轨迹多样性并作为奖励信号，同时结合成功条件约束（success-conditioning）防止无效动作被奖励。Proposing the DRIVE framework, which uses Gaussian Kernel (GAK) to calculate trajectory diversity as a reward signal, while combining success-conditioning constraints to prevent rewarding invalid actions.
推进: Advance: 揭示了RFT过程中“全局收缩但成功模式发散”的规律，显著提升了模型在分布外（OOD）场景下的泛化能力，为VLA从单一模仿向多样化自主决策演进提供了新路径。Revealing the pattern of 'global contraction but divergence of successful patterns' during RFT, significantly improving the model's generalization capability in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios, and providing a new path for VLA evolution from single imitation to diverse autonomous decision-making.

人机回环与混合策略Human-in-the-loop and hybrid strategies

该方向旨在解决纯强化学习在真实具身场景中训练效率低、安全性差及泛化难的问题，核心思路是引入人机回环（HIL）与混合策略，将人类专家的知识、实时干预或演示数据作为强先验与纠错机制。演进脉络从早期依赖高频人工纠正（如HIL-SERL），逐步转向利用一致性策略、离线经验修正及自动化监督机制（如ConRFT、π*0.6、AutoSERL），试图在保留人类安全兜底的同时，降低对人工实时干预的依赖，实现从“人机共驾”向“半自主进化”的务实过渡。This direction aims to address the low training efficiency, poor safety, and difficult generalization of pure reinforcement learning in real embodied scenarios. The core idea is to introduce Human-in-the-Loop (HIL) and hybrid strategies, incorporating human expert knowledge, real-time intervention, or demonstration data as strong priors and error-correction mechanisms. The evolutionary trajectory has shifted from early reliance on high-frequency human correction (e.g., HIL-SERL) to utilizing consistency policies, offline experience correction, and automated supervision mechanisms (e.g., ConRFT, π*0.6, AutoSERL), attempting to maintain human safety fallback while reducing dependence on real-time human intervention, achieving a pragmatic transition from "human-machine co-driving" to "semi-autonomous evolution."

方法: Method: 提出HuMBLE框架，通过蒸馏技术将人类运动数据转化为策略先验，并结合多任务强化学习将提取的步态特征嵌入控制回路。Proposing the HuMBLE framework, which distills human motion data into policy priors via distillation techniques and embeds extracted gait features into the control loop by combining multi-task reinforcement learning.
推进: Advance: 实现了在Atlas和G1等真实机器人上的快速部署，证明了混合人类先验与RL训练能显著提升运动自然度与实机鲁棒性，减少了纯仿真训练的域鸿沟。Achieving rapid deployment on real robots such as Atlas and G1, demonstrating that hybrid human priors combined with RL training can significantly improve movement naturalness and real-robot robustness, reducing the domain gap of pure simulation training.

仿真到现实的物理鸿沟与触觉感知Sim-to-Real physical gap and tactile perception

该方向聚焦于解决具身智能中 Sim2Real 的物理鸿沟与触觉感知迁移难题。核心思路从早期的单纯域随机化，演进为利用残差模型修正动力学偏差（如 ASAP），或构建高效触觉仿真器（如 FOTS）以低成本获取高保真接触数据。近期工作进一步探索无传感器力控（如 FACET, PressMimic）及底层执行器补偿（如 Actuator Reality Shaping），试图通过“感知 - 控制”解耦或物理模型修正来填补仿真与现实的差距。演进趋势是从依赖昂贵传感器转向利用本体感知与算法补偿，从通用策略转向针对接触密集任务的精细化物理建模与零样本部署。This direction focuses on solving the Sim2Real physical gap and tactile perception transfer challenges in embodied intelligence. The core idea has evolved from early simple domain randomization to utilizing residual models to correct dynamic biases (e.g., ASAP), or constructing efficient tactile simulators (e.g., FOTS) to obtain high-fidelity contact data at low cost. Recent work further explores sensorless force control (e.g., FACET, PressMimic) and bottom-level actuator compensation (e.g., Actuator Reality Shaping), attempting to bridge the gap between simulation and reality through "perception-control" decoupling or physical model correction. The evolutionary trend shifts from relying on expensive sensors to utilizing proprioception and algorithmic compensation, and from general policies to fine-grained physical modeling and zero-shot deployment for contact-intensive tasks.

推进: Advance: 显著降低了触觉 Sim2Real 的算力门槛，在 peg-in-hole 任务中验证了简化物理模型在特定接触场景下的有效性，缩短了真机调试周期。Significantly lower the computational threshold for tactile Sim2Real, validating the effectiveness of simplified physical models in specific contact scenarios in peg-in-hole tasks, shortening the real-robot debugging cycle.

ComplexMimic: Human-Scene Interaction Imitation in Complex 3D Environments
动机: Motivation: 虚拟人在复杂 3D 场景中易发生穿模且动作不自然，需将“像不像”与“能不能”拆解训练以提升交互质量。Virtual humans are prone to mesh penetration and unnatural movements in complex 3D scenarios, requiring the decoupled training of 'looks-like' and 'can-do' to improve interaction quality.
方法: Method: 提出 ComplexMimic，采用双专家架构分别优化动作自然度与物理可行性，并通过蒸馏实现高效部署。Proposed ComplexMimic, adopting a dual-expert architecture to optimize action naturalness and physical feasibility separately, and achieving efficient deployment through distillation.
推进: Advance: 在复杂家具环境中显著提升了无穿模成功率与动作精度，证明了分而治之策略在复杂物理交互中的有效性。Significantly improved mesh-penetration-free success rates and action accuracy in complex furniture environments, proving the effectiveness of divide-and-conquer strategies in complex physical interactions.

A Minimalist Retargeting-Guided Reinforcement Learning Recipe for Dexterous Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对灵巧操作中传统运动学重定向忽略接触物理约束导致仿真策略无法迁移至现实的痛点，旨在通过交互感知的重定向降低Sim2Real鸿沟。Aiming to address the pain point that traditional kinematic retargeting in dexterous manipulation ignores contact physical constraints, leading to the inability of simulated policies to transfer to reality, this work seeks to bridge the Sim2Real gap through interaction-aware retargeting.
方法: Method: 提出REGRIND框架，将人类动作重定向至机器人时显式考虑物体接触状态，并结合强化学习进行微调以修正动力学偏差。We propose the REGRIND framework, which explicitly considers object contact states when retargeting human actions to robots and combines reinforcement learning for fine-tuning to correct dynamic biases.
推进: Advance: 证明了在接触密集任务中保持交互语义比单纯模仿运动学轨迹更能提升策略的鲁棒性，为工程落地提供了无需昂贵传感器的实用配方。We prove that maintaining interaction semantics, rather than merely imitating kinematic trajectories, enhances policy robustness in contact-intensive tasks, providing a practical recipe for engineering deployment that does not require expensive sensors.

推进: Advance: 验证了强化学习在复杂滚动接触任务中的迁移潜力，但暴露了简化接触模型在真机速度跟踪上仍存在显著增益偏差的局限性。We validate the transfer potential of reinforcement learning in complex rolling contact tasks, but expose the limitations of simplified contact models, which still exhibit significant gain bias in true machine speed tracking.

动机: Motivation: 指出当前 RL 在 Sim2Real 迁移中过度关注算法收敛，而忽视了真实世界中实时延迟与抖动引发的跨层系统失效。Pointed out that current RL in Sim2Real transfer overly focuses on algorithmic convergence while neglecting cross-layer system failures caused by real-time latency and jitter in the real world.
方法: Method: 构建包含物理、网络、执行三层的量化框架，将延迟分布与性能翻转（Performance Flip）纳入设计约束进行系统分析。Constructed a quantitative framework encompassing physical, network, and execution layers, incorporating latency distribution and performance flip into design constraints for system analysis.
推进: Advance: 颠覆了“仿真快即现实快”的迷思，确立了实时性作为 Sim2Real 系统工程的核心约束而非实现细节，为高容错任务提供避坑指南。Subverted the myth that 'simulation speed equals real-world speed', establishing real-time performance as a core constraint of Sim2Real systems engineering rather than an implementation detail, providing a guide to avoid pitfalls for high fault-tolerance tasks.

Effective Parameters, Real Behavior: Renormalization for Robotics -- From Infinite Electron Mass to Sim-to-Real Gap
动机: Motivation: 针对仿真中因离散化频率导致的惯性、阻尼等参数失效问题，旨在解决高保真物理模型难以直接迁移至现实硬件的鸿沟。Aiming to address the issue of parameter failure for inertia, damping, etc., caused by discretization frequency in simulations, and to bridge the gap where high-fidelity physical models are difficult to directly transfer to real-world hardware.
方法: Method: 引入量子场论中的“重整化”概念，通过PD控制推导将仿真频率下的参数映射为现实中的“有效参数”，以此补偿离散化误差。Introducing the concept of 'renormalization' from quantum field theory, deriving PD control to map parameters at simulation frequency to 'effective parameters' in reality, thereby compensating for discretization errors.
推进: Advance: 突破了单纯追求仿真精度的传统思路，提出在计算资源受限场景下通过调整“有效参数”而非修改模型结构来实现Sim2Real迁移的新范式。Breaking through the traditional approach of solely pursuing simulation accuracy, proposing a new paradigm for Sim2Real transfer under computationally constrained scenarios by adjusting 'effective parameters' rather than modifying model structures.

动机: Motivation: 刚性体仿真难以准确模拟弹性体接触，需回归物理本质利用 FEM 解决接触密集任务的 Sim2Real 难题。Rigid body simulation struggles to accurately model elastic contact, necessitating a return to physical fundamentals using FEM to solve Sim2Real challenges in contact-intensive tasks.
方法: Method: 提出基于 IPC 的 FEM 仿真协议，结合 Marker-based 传感器与自监督预训练，实现高保真接触物理建模。Proposed an FEM simulation protocol based on IPC, combining Marker-based sensors and self-supervised pre-training to achieve high-fidelity contact physics modeling.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了弹性体形变的仿真精度，为接触任务提供了更物理一致的训练环境，但计算开销限制了其实时交互能力。Significantly improved simulation accuracy for elastic deformation, providing a more physically consistent training environment for contact tasks, yet computational overhead limits real-time interaction capabilities.

世界模型与幻觉治理World Models and Hallucination Governance

该方向聚焦于利用世界模型赋能强化学习，核心在于解决模型幻觉、数据覆盖不足及奖励黑客三大顽疾。演进脉络从早期依赖上帝视角的规划（Think2Drive），转向通过数据工程与多模态诊断根治幻觉（Hallucination, MoP-JEPA, iKCE）。随后，研究重心扩展至构建动态评估体系以抑制奖励黑客（Reward as Agent），并通过自适应想象与反事实生成填补Sim-to-Real数据鸿沟（AIDA, WorldSample）。最新进展引入控制理论诊断模型能控性（CROF）及物理一致性约束（VAORA），标志着世界模型从“生成式黑盒”向“可验证、可解释、物理对齐”的具身智能基石转变。This direction focuses on empowering reinforcement learning with world models, centering on solving three major ailments: model hallucinations, insufficient data coverage, and reward hacking. The evolutionary trajectory has shifted from early planning relying on a 'God's eye view' (Think2Drive) to rooting out hallucinations through data engineering and multimodal diagnosis (Hallucination, MoP-JEPA, iKCE). Subsequently, research focus expanded to building dynamic evaluation systems to suppress reward hacking (Reward as Agent), and utilizing adaptive imagination and counterfactual generation to bridge the Sim-to-Real data gap (AIDA, WorldSample). Latest advancements introduced control theory-based diagnosis of model controllability (CROF) and physical consistency constraints (VAORA), marking a transformation of world models from 'generative black boxes' to the cornerstone of embodied intelligence that is 'verifiable, interpretable, and physically aligned'.

方法: Method: 构建大规模视频数据集MMBench2，提出基于数据覆盖度的幻觉分类与预防策略，验证小模型通过数据增强可抑制幻觉。Constructing the large-scale video dataset MMBench2, we propose hallucination classification and prevention strategies based on data coverage, verifying that small models can suppress hallucinations through data augmentation.
推进: Advance: 将幻觉问题从玄学拉回数据工程，证明数据多样性是解决模型预测偏差的关键，为小参数模型具身智能提供新路径。Returning the hallucination problem from metaphysics to data engineering, we prove that data diversity is key to resolving model prediction bias, offering a new path for embodied intelligence in small-parameter models.

方法: Method: 提出Koopman Dreamer，利用Koopman算子理论对潜在动力学施加谱约束，替代传统的非线性神经网络预测。We propose Koopman Dreamer, utilizing Koopman operator theory to impose spectral constraints on latent dynamics, replacing traditional nonlinear neural network prediction.

From Generation to Simulation: How Far Are World Models from Being True Simulators?
动机: Motivation: 批判当前生成式世界模型盲目追求“模拟器”地位，却缺乏物理定律保证和结构化状态反馈，导致其本质仍是不可验证的黑盒。Critiques current generative world models for blindly pursuing the status of 'simulators' while lacking guarantees of physical laws and structured state feedback, resulting in them essentially remaining unverifiable black boxes.
方法: Method: 建立传统模拟器能力审计清单，对200多篇相关论文进行系统性评估，重点考察运行时状态接口暴露与物理一致性。Establishes an audit checklist for traditional simulator capabilities, systematically evaluating over 200 related papers, with a focus on runtime state interface exposure and physical consistency.
推进: Advance: 揭示了生成式模型在可控性和交互性上的巨大鸿沟，指出仅有6篇论文暴露了运行时状态接口，为从“生成”转向“可验证模拟”提供了关键诊断依据。Reveals a huge gap in controllability and interactivity among generative models, noting that only 6 papers expose runtime state interfaces, providing key diagnostic basis for shifting from 'generation' to 'verifiable simulation'.

WorldReward: Reward Modeling for Camera-Conditioned World Models
动机: Motivation: 针对相机条件世界模型在长视频评估中因上下文过长导致的幻觉与奖励信号失真问题，旨在构建一个能抵御“晕车”效应的精准裁判。Aiming to address the hallucination and reward signal distortion caused by excessively long context in long video evaluation of camera-conditioned world models, this work seeks to build a precise judge that can resist the 'motion sickness' effect.
方法: Method: 提出WorldReward框架，利用VLM将长视频切分为局部动作块进行独立评估，并通过蒸馏大模型数据与人类标注结合来统一视觉质量与动作一致性。Proposes the WorldReward framework, which uses a VLM to segment long videos into local action blocks for independent evaluation, and unifies visual quality and action consistency by distilling large model data combined with human annotations.
推进: Advance: 有效解决了长序列下的奖励黑客与幻觉累积问题，显著提升了HY-WorldPlay在RL微调中的稳定性，证明了工程化奖励建模在抑制模型幻觉上的有效性。Effectively solves the problems of reward hacking and hallucination accumulation under long sequences, significantly improving the stability of HY-WorldPlay in RL fine-tuning, proving the effectiveness of engineering-based reward modeling in suppressing model hallucinations.

WISE: World-model-guided Imagination Scheduling for Efficient Post-training of Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 为了解决视觉语言模型（VLA）后训练阶段算力消耗巨大且盲目试错效率低下的问题，需引入世界模型指导的想象调度机制。To address the massive computational consumption and low efficiency of blind trial-and-error in the post-training phase of vision-language models (VLA), an imagination scheduling mechanism guided by world models is required.
方法: Method: 设计WISE框架，利用世界模型生成的视觉特征动态调度想象过程，仅在关键决策点触发高成本推理，从而大幅降低算力需求。Designs the WISE framework, which utilizes visual features generated by the world model to dynamically schedule the imagination process, triggering high-cost reasoning only at critical decision points, thereby significantly reducing computational power requirements.
推进: Advance: 在减少80%算力的同时实现了26%的泛化成功率提升，证实了基于世界模型的“预知规划”比传统试错更能有效治理因数据覆盖不足导致的策略幻觉。Achieves a 26% improvement in generalization success rate while reducing computational power by 80%, confirming that world model-based 'foreknowledge planning' is more effective than traditional trial-and-error in governing strategy hallucinations caused by insufficient data coverage.

Long-Horizon Consistent and Interaction-Aware World Models for Multi-Style End-to-End Driving
动机: Motivation: 针对端到端自动驾驶中长时交互场景下模型幻觉导致的策略不一致及极端场景泛化失败问题，需强化世界模型的长时一致性约束。To address strategy inconsistency and generalization failure in extreme scenarios caused by model hallucinations in long-term interaction scenarios in end-to-end autonomous driving, it is necessary to strengthen the long-term consistency constraints of world models.
方法: Method: 构建StyleDrive模型，引入长时一致性正则、交互解耦机制及多风格策略优化（基于GRPO），以平衡保守与激进驾驶行为。Constructs the StyleDrive model, introducing long-term consistency regularization, interaction decoupling mechanisms, and multi-style policy optimization (based on GRPO) to balance conservative and aggressive driving behaviors.
推进: Advance: 在Bench2Drive基准上显著超越基线，证明了通过多风格奖励加权与一致性约束能有效抑制长视界下的误差累积，为WM在真实物理场景中的落地提供了新范式。Significantly outperforms baselines on the Bench2Drive benchmark, proving that multi-style reward weighting and consistency constraints can effectively suppress error accumulation under long horizons, providing a new paradigm for the deployment of WM in real physical scenarios.

GPU-Accelerated Astrodynamics World Models for Spacecraft Rendezvous and Proximity Operations
动机: Motivation: 面对航天器交会对接中传统滤波方法难以处理高维非线性扰动及仿真数据稀缺的问题，需利用GPU加速的世界模型构建高保真物理想象空间。Facing the problem that traditional filtering methods struggle to handle high-dimensional nonlinear perturbations and the scarcity of simulation data in spacecraft rendezvous and docking, it is necessary to utilize GPU-accelerated world models to build a high-fidelity physical imagination space.
方法: Method: 基于JAX构建GPU加速的天体力学世界模型，利用Transformer在海量仿真数据上学习轨道动力学，实现未来观测预测与异常检测。Builds a GPU-accelerated celestial mechanics world model based on JAX, utilizing Transformers to learn orbital dynamics on massive simulation data, achieving future observation prediction and anomaly detection.
推进: Advance: 在仿真环境中实现了98%的异常检测准确率，验证了数据驱动的世界模型在复杂物理约束下比纯RL更具备可解释性与抗干扰能力，为Sim-to-Real鸿沟填补提供了新思路。Achieves a 98% accuracy rate in anomaly detection in simulation environments, verifying that data-driven world models possess better interpretability and anti-interference capabilities than pure RL under complex physical constraints, providing new ideas for bridging the Sim-to-Real gap.

推进: Advance: 在Unitree G1真机上实现了93.3%的成功率，彻底解决了传统RL在复杂地形下的0%失效问题，证明了轻量级世界模型能有效治理数据覆盖不足引发的策略幻觉。Achieves a 93.3% success rate on the Unitree G1 real robot, completely solving the 0% failure problem of traditional RL in complex terrain, proving that lightweight world models can effectively govern strategy hallucinations caused by insufficient data coverage.

HaWMPO: Hallucination-Aware World Model-based Policy Optimization for Generalist Robot Policy
动机: Motivation: 针对世界模型强化学习中生成数据存在严重幻觉导致策略在虚拟环境中“走火入魔”的问题，亟需一种机制来量化并抑制不可靠的生成样本。Addressing the problem of severe hallucinations in generated data within world model reinforcement learning causing policies to 'go astray' in virtual environments, urgently requiring a mechanism to quantify and suppress unreliable generated samples.
方法: Method: 提出幻觉感知模块（HAM）实时监控生成轨迹的置信度，并引入软惩罚机制（Reward-Soft）对高幻觉风险的数据进行降权处理。Proposing a Hallucination-Aware Module (HAM) to monitor the confidence of generated trajectories in real-time, and introducing a soft penalty mechanism (Reward-Soft) to down-weight data with high hallucination risks.
推进: Advance: 在LIBERO和真机G1上验证了该方法能有效提升策略成功率，为具身智能的“想象空间”训练提供了可落地的“刹车片”，但依赖人工指标定义的局限性仍需关注。Validated on LIBERO and real robot G1 that this method effectively improves policy success rates, providing a deployable 'brake pad' for embodied intelligence 'imagination space' training, though limitations dependent on manually defined metrics require attention.

Proof-Carrying Cognition: Closing the Verification Gap with Reality-Settled Reward
动机: Motivation: 为了解决开放域推理中因奖励黑客导致的模型“刷分”现象，需要一种将推理过程转化为可证伪赌注并基于现实进行结算的机制。To address the model 'score farming' phenomenon caused by reward hacking in open-domain reasoning, a mechanism is needed to transform the reasoning process into falsifiable bets and settle them based on reality.
方法: Method: 提出“携带证明的认知”（Proof-Carrying Cognition）框架，利用世界模型对推理进行定价，并通过预注册复现机制强制模型接受现实数据的结算。Proposing a 'Proof-Carrying Cognition' framework, utilizing world models to price reasoning and enforcing model acceptance of reality data settlement through a pre-registered replication mechanism.
推进: Advance: 揭示了现有世界模型多为浅层回归器且理论假设（如高斯分布）在现实非高斯域中失效的脆弱性，将研究重心从单纯提升性能转向构建防欺骗的方法论。Revealing the fragility of existing world models as shallow regressors and the failure of theoretical assumptions (e.g., Gaussian distributions) in real non-Gaussian domains, shifting research focus from simply improving performance to building anti-deception methodologies.

方法: Method: 构建紧凑的视觉触觉世界模型，通过对比实验发现简单的状态保持策略在力预测指标上优于复杂模型，并深入分析校准后的轨迹覆盖率与实际执行成功率的错位。Constructing a compact visual-tactile world model, discovering through comparative experiments that simple state-holding strategies outperform complex models in force prediction metrics, and deeply analyzing the misalignment between calibrated trajectory coverage and actual execution success rates.

动机: Motivation: 针对当前世界模型过度追求视觉逼真度而忽视物理逻辑与决策效用，导致幻觉频发且无法指导具身行动的问题。Addresses the issue where current world models overly pursue visual fidelity while neglecting physical logic and decision utility, leading to frequent hallucinations and an inability to guide embodied actions.

推进: Advance: 确立了从生成式黑盒向可验证、可解释的具身智能基石转变的范式，揭示了仅靠视觉保真度无法解决奖励黑客与规划失效的根本原因。Establishes a paradigm shift from generative black boxes to verifiable and interpretable foundations of embodied intelligence, revealing that visual fidelity alone cannot solve the root causes of reward hacking and planning failure.

When Should a World Model Move? Loss-Conditioned State Execution
动机: Motivation: 打破“预测即行动”的迷思，解决模型因过度自信或高方差预测而导致的无效状态更新与幻觉驱动的错误决策。Breaks the myth of 'prediction is action', solving ineffective state updates and hallucination-driven erroneous decisions caused by models being overconfident or having high-variance predictions.
方法: Method: 设计损失条件执行（Loss-Conditioned State Execution）机制，通过统计检验仅在提案能显著降低绝对损失时才允许状态更新，否则强制保持现状。Designs a Loss-Conditioned State Execution mechanism, allowing state updates only when proposals can significantly reduce absolute loss through statistical testing; otherwise, the status quo is forcibly maintained.
推进: Advance: 证明了在特定损失函数下，预测分布的方差与概率并非行动依据，为抑制模型幻觉引发的盲目探索提供了严格的理论边界与安全过滤策略。Proves that under specific loss functions, the variance and probability of the prediction distribution are not bases for action, providing strict theoretical boundaries and safety filtering strategies to suppress blind exploration triggered by model hallucinations.

Calibration-risk routing for controlled world-model adaptation

安全与离线强化学习Safety and Offline Reinforcement Learning

在安全与离线强化学习视角下，核心矛盾在于如何在不进行高风险在线探索的前提下，确保智能体在复杂环境中严格满足安全约束。演进脉络从早期的软约束期望优化，转向基于可达性分析的硬约束保障，如利用哈密顿 - 雅可比方程划定安全边界。同时，研究开始融合模仿学习与离线数据，通过理论证明行为克隆在特定假设下的样本效率，并引入扩散模型等生成式方法处理可行性问题。此外，工作重心逐渐从纯算法优化转向工程落地，强调数据质量、CAD 先验及训练过程的有界控制，力求在离线数据受限或存在偏差时，仍能实现鲁棒且安全的决策。From the perspective of safety and offline reinforcement learning, the core contradiction lies in ensuring that agents strictly satisfy safety constraints in complex environments without high-risk online exploration. The evolutionary trajectory has shifted from early soft-constraint expectation optimization to hard-constraint guarantees based on reachability analysis, such as using Hamilton-Jacobi equations to delineate safety boundaries. Simultaneously, research has begun integrating imitation learning and offline data, theoretically proving the sample efficiency of behavior cloning under specific assumptions, and introducing generative methods like diffusion models to handle feasibility issues. Furthermore, the focus has gradually shifted from pure algorithmic optimization to engineering deployment, emphasizing data quality, CAD priors, and bounded control of the training process, striving to achieve robust and safe decision-making even when offline data is limited or biased.

动机: Motivation: 为加速离线数据利用并减少实机训练中的探索风险，试图利用物理对称性对轨迹数据进行超缩放以增强策略鲁棒性。Sought to accelerate offline data utilization and reduce exploration risks in real-world training by leveraging physical symmetries to super-scale trajectory data and enhance policy robustness.

推进: Advance: 证实了利用对称性可在离线数据受限下实现训练速度倍增，但揭示了外视角几何变换在复杂场景下易产生伪影，强调了数据增强在安全边界内的适用性限制。Confirmed that leveraging symmetries can double training speed under offline data constraints, but revealed that outer-view geometric transformations are prone to artifacts in complex scenarios, emphasizing the applicability limits of data augmentation within safety boundaries.

推进: Advance: 证明了在离线数据受限场景下，利用未来状态信息作为监督信号可显著提升样本效率与行为克隆的鲁棒性，为离线GUI智能体在无法实时验证环境反馈时的安全决策提供了新的数据利用范式。Demonstrates that in offline data-limited scenarios, utilizing future state information as a supervision signal can significantly improve sample efficiency and the robustness of behavior cloning, providing a new data utilization paradigm for safe decision-making of offline GUI agents when real-time environmental feedback verification is unavailable.

RT-Safe: Benchmarking Agent Safety in Real-Time Embodied Environment
动机: Motivation: 揭露具身智能中任务完成率高不等于安全的泡沫，指出推理延迟本身在动态物理环境中即构成安全风险。Exposing the bubble that high task completion rates in embodied intelligence do not equate to safety, pointing out that inference latency itself constitutes a safety risk in dynamic physical environments.
方法: Method: 构建 RT-SAFE 基准测试，在实时具身环境中评估模型在动态交互下的安全表现，对比传统冻结世界基准。Constructing the RT-SAFE benchmark to evaluate model safety performance under dynamic interactions in real-time embodied environments, comparing it with traditional frozen world benchmarks.
推进: Advance: 实证发现增加推理时间反而导致碰撞增加，证明延迟是物理风险源，打破了传统安全评估忽略时间动态性的局限。Empirically finding that increasing inference time leads to increased collisions, proving that latency is a physical risk source and breaking the limitations of traditional safety assessments that ignore temporal dynamics.

算法优化与训练技巧Algorithm Optimization and Training Techniques

该方向聚焦于通过算法优化与训练技巧解决强化学习在具身智能中的收敛慢、样本效率低及泛化差等核心痛点。演进脉络从早期的归一化与学习率动态调整（如NaP），转向利用动作分块、经验回放及轨迹优化混合策略提升探索效率与数据利用率。近期研究更强调推理时的轻量化引导（如Q-guided、最优传输）及特征解耦以增强泛化，同时尝试用LLM辅助训练监控。整体趋势是从单纯堆砌算力转向精细化的训练动力学控制、工程化技巧融合及推理加速，旨在降低对真实环境交互的依赖并提升策略的鲁棒性。This direction focuses on addressing core pain points in embodied intelligence reinforcement learning, such as slow convergence, low sample efficiency, and poor generalization, through algorithm optimization and training techniques. The evolutionary trajectory has shifted from early normalization and dynamic learning rate adjustments (e.g., NaP) to leveraging action chunking, experience replay, and trajectory optimization mixed strategies to enhance exploration efficiency and data utilization. Recent research emphasizes lightweight guidance during inference (e.g., Q-guided, optimal transport) and feature disentanglement to improve generalization, while also attempting to use LLMs for training monitoring. The overall trend is shifting from simply piling up computational power to refined training dynamics control, integration of engineering techniques, and inference acceleration, aiming to reduce dependence on real-environment interaction and enhance policy robustness.

Stop Regressing: Training Value Functions via Classification for Scalable Deep RL
动机: Motivation: 针对深度强化学习中价值函数训练采用均方误差（MSE）回归导致大模型（如Transformer）训练不稳定且效果不佳的痛点，指出回归任务在扩展性上的局限性。Addressing the pain point that mean squared error (MSE) regression in value function training for deep reinforcement learning leads to unstable training and poor performance in large models (e.g., Transformers), this work points out the limitations of regression tasks in scalability.
方法: Method: 提出将价值函数训练转化为分类问题，利用交叉熵损失函数替代传统的MSE回归损失，从而适配大规模架构。Proposing to transform value function training into a classification problem, utilizing cross-entropy loss functions to replace traditional MSE regression losses, thereby adapting to large-scale architectures.
推进: Advance: 在无需改变策略架构的前提下，显著提升了Atari、机器人及棋类游戏等大模型场景下的训练稳定性与最终性能，实现了零成本的算法升级。Significantly improving training stability and final performance in large-model scenarios such as Atari, robotics, and board games without altering the policy architecture, achieving zero-cost algorithm upgrades.

动机: Motivation: 针对归一化在持续学习中导致参数范数膨胀及有效学习率隐形衰减的问题，旨在解决训练动力学的稳定性。Addresses the issue of parameter norm explosion and implicit learning rate decay caused by normalization in continual learning, aiming to ensure the stability of training dynamics.
方法: Method: 提出Normalize-and-Project (NaP) 方法，将隐式的学习率衰减转化为显式控制，并配合优化器层面的调整。Proposed the Normalize-and-Project (NaP) method, converting implicit learning rate decay into explicit control and coordinating with adjustments at the optimizer level.
推进: Advance: 理论证明了归一化副作用的机制，在静态任务中避免了性能退化，为RL训练动力学提供了新的控制视角。Theoretically proved the mechanism of normalization side effects, avoided performance degradation in static tasks, and provided a new control perspective for RL training dynamics.

Sharpen Before You Adapt: Data-Free Entry-State Sharpening for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning
动机: Motivation: 指出测试时强化学习（TTRL）中模型初始策略分布过散（高熵）会导致优化步数浪费，阻碍收敛效率。Pointing out that an overly dispersed initial policy distribution (high entropy) in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning (TTRL) leads to wasted optimization steps, hindering convergence efficiency.
方法: Method: 提出“先锐化后适应”策略，通过无数据状态锐化技术（如SPIRAL）在测试前压缩策略分布，降低进入状态的熵。Proposing a 'sharpen then adapt' strategy, compressing the policy distribution before testing via data-free state sharpening techniques (such as SPIRAL) to reduce the entropy of the entry state.

Q-Learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching
动机: Motivation: 针对Flow Policy在RL微调中计算标量伴随（Scalar Adjoint）时，传统向量-雅可比积（VJP）导致的高昂计算成本问题，旨在为资源受限场景提供轻量级优化方案。Aiming to provide a lightweight optimization solution for resource-constrained scenarios by addressing the high computational cost of traditional vector-Jacobian products (VJP) when computing scalar adjoints for Flow Policy in RL fine-tuning.
方法: Method: 利用预训练Flow策略雅可比矩阵的对角线特性，假设其各向同性，将昂贵的逐层反向传播推导为闭式标量缩放，并引入针对动作生成的价值惩罚机制以弥补近似误差。Leveraging the diagonal characteristics of the pre-trained Flow policy Jacobian matrix, assuming isotropy, to derive closed-form scalar scaling from expensive layer-by-layer backpropagation, and introducing a value penalty mechanism for action generation to compensate for approximation errors.
推进: Advance: 成功将计算复杂度从向量级降至标量级，在OGBench复杂任务上实现18-35%的性能提升并验证了真机VLA微调的可行性，展示了工程直觉在加速训练动力学方面的有效性。Successfully reducing computational complexity from vector-level to scalar-level, achieving a 18-35% performance improvement on OGBench complex tasks and verifying the feasibility of real-robot VLA fine-tuning, demonstrating the effectiveness of engineering intuition in accelerating training dynamics.

具身大模型与策略架构创新Embodied Large Models and Policy Architecture Innovation

本方向聚焦于突破具身大模型（VLA）在复杂物理环境中的策略泛化与实时响应瓶颈。核心思路从单纯依赖海量数据预训练，转向架构轻量化、多模态深度融合（触觉/空间）及动态推理机制的创新。演进路径上，早期工作致力于将语言推理与物理动作解耦，随后通过引入神经符号验证、空间工具内化及预测性触觉建模，解决模型在长程规划与细粒度操作中的幻觉与延迟问题。最新进展强调离线/在线混合微调与自适应RL机制，旨在以低成本参数实现高鲁棒性，平衡推理能力与系统实时性，推动具身智能从仿真走向真实部署。This direction focuses on breaking through the bottlenecks of strategy generalization and real-time response of embodied large models (VLA) in complex physical environments. The core approach shifts from solely relying on massive data pre-training to innovations in architecture lightweighting, deep multimodal fusion (tactile/spatial), and dynamic reasoning mechanisms. In terms of evolutionary path, early work aimed to decouple language reasoning from physical actions, followed by introducing neuro-symbolic verification, spatial tool internalization, and predictive tactile modeling to address hallucinations and delays in long-horizon planning and fine-grained manipulation. Latest advancements emphasize offline/online hybrid fine-tuning and adaptive RL mechanisms, aiming to achieve high robustness with low-cost parameters, balancing reasoning capabilities with system real-time performance, and promoting embodied intelligence from simulation to real-world deployment.

推进: Advance: 实现了从仿真到实机的斜坡自适应全身运动控制，证明了通过显式物理约束而非单纯数据驱动可显著提升长程规划中的细粒度操作鲁棒性。Achieving slope-adaptive whole-body motion control from simulation to real robots, demonstrating that explicit physical constraints, rather than purely data-driven approaches, can significantly enhance fine-grained operational robustness in long-horizon planning.
2026-09
A Mathematical Model of Motivated Emotional Mind - Cognitive Embodied System
动机: Motivation: 为了解决传统强化学习中静态奖励函数难以应对动态环境变化及长程规划中“幻觉”与延迟的问题，试图从认知架构层面重构具身智能的决策驱动力。To address the difficulty of static reward functions in traditional reinforcement learning in coping with dynamic environmental changes and the issues of 'hallucinations' and delays in long-horizon planning, this work attempts to reconstruct the decision-driving force of embodied intelligence from the cognitive architecture level.
方法: Method: 构建基于动机与稳态（allostasis）的数学模型MEM，利用“似元”（semblions）绑定感觉与身体状态，并通过再入机制实现情感作为调节信号的内化。Constructing a mathematical model MEM based on motivation and allostasis, utilizing 'semblions' to bind sensory and bodily states, and achieving the internalization of emotions as regulatory signals through re-entrant mechanisms.
推进: Advance: 提出了一种不依赖外部奖励函数的内在驱动学习范式，为具身大模型在复杂物理环境中实现自适应动态推理提供了新的理论架构支撑。Proposing an intrinsic-driven learning paradigm that does not rely on external reward functions, providing new theoretical architectural support for embodied large models to achieve adaptive dynamic reasoning in complex physical environments.
2026-09
Learning Expressive and Compositional Motion Representation via Spectral Skills
动机: Motivation: 针对传统高维规划器直接预测关节轨迹导致的数据效率低且泛化能力差的问题，旨在突破具身大模型在复杂物理环境中的运动表征瓶颈。Addressing the issues of low data efficiency and poor generalization caused by traditional high-dimensional planners directly predicting joint trajectories, aiming to break through the motion representation bottleneck of embodied large models in complex physical environments.
方法: Method: 提出谱技能（Spectral Skills）框架，将运动表征从被动的数据重建转变为基于谱方向组合的未来动作预测，实现无需重训的动作零样本组合。Proposing the Spectral Skills framework, transforming motion representation from passive data reconstruction to future action prediction based on spectral direction combinations, achieving zero-shot action composition without retraining.
推进: Advance: 通过解耦运动基元实现了全身控制的灵活组合，显著降低了轨迹预测误差，证明了预测性表征在长程规划与细粒度操作中的优越性。Achieving flexible combination of whole-body control by decoupling motion primitives, significantly reducing trajectory prediction error, and proving the superiority of predictive representations in long-horizon planning and fine-grained manipulation.
2026-10
EmbodiedRSI: Active Continual Robot Learning Through Hypothesis-Guided Co-Evolution
动机: Motivation: 针对具身大模型在真实物理环境中因分布偏移导致的策略幻觉与高成本试错问题，旨在突破单纯依赖海量数据预训练的局限。Aiming to break the limitations of relying solely on massive data pre-training to address strategy hallucinations and high-cost trial-and-error caused by distribution shifts in embodied large models within real physical environments.
方法: Method: 提出假设引导的协同进化架构，将代码逻辑与技能表征绑定，通过快慢系统实现基于物理试错的主动持续学习。Proposing a hypothesis-guided co-evolution architecture that binds code logic with skill representations, enabling active continuous learning based on physical trial-and-error through a fast-slow system.
推进: Advance: 实现了仿真到真机的零样本迁移，在复杂任务中达到71.3%的成功率，证明了通过动态假设更新可有效缓解长程规划中的延迟与幻觉。Achieving zero-shot transfer from simulation to real-robot, reaching a 71.3% success rate on complex tasks, proving that dynamic hypothesis updates can effectively mitigate latency and hallucinations in long-horizon planning.
2026-10
Energy-Efficient Gait Adaptation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Diverse Terrains
动机: Motivation: 为了解决现有端到端策略在能耗优化与突发地形适应之间的权衡难题，需构建兼顾高层推理与底层实时控制的轻量化架构。Addressing the trade-off dilemma between energy optimization and sudden terrain adaptation in existing end-to-end policies, requiring the construction of a lightweight architecture that balances high-level reasoning and low-level real-time control.
方法: Method: 设计分层强化学习框架，利用思维链（CoT）在高层进行能耗导向的长程规划，底层则专注于高频率的步态稳定性控制。Designing a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that utilizes Chain-of-Thought (CoT) for energy-oriented long-horizon planning at the high level, while the low level focuses on high-frequency gait stability control.
推进: Advance: 在AlienGo四足机器人上实现零样本真机部署，显著降低能耗并支持多速度自适应切换，验证了神经符号验证在物理约束下的鲁棒性。Achieving zero-shot real-robot deployment on the AlienGo quadruped robot, significantly reducing energy consumption and supporting multi-speed adaptive switching, verifying the robustness of neuro-symbolic verification under physical constraints.

身体即计算与神经形态控制Body as Computation and Neuromorphic Control

在“身体即计算与神经形态控制”视角下，强化学习正从单纯优化控制器参数转向协同设计身体结构与神经机制。该方向核心思路是利用物理系统的动力学特性（如肌肉非线性、被动稳定性）分担计算压力，实现极简神经架构。演进路径上，早期研究多依赖传统网络配合复杂物理仿真，而近期工作（如#2）提出将身体视为感知器、物理参数视为权重，通过神经形态控制与肌肉网络协同优化，以极少神经元实现复杂运动。这标志着具身智能从“大脑主导”向“身心融合”的范式转变，旨在解决高维控制难题并提升系统鲁棒性，尽管目前仍面临仿真到现实迁移及优化效率等工程挑战。From the perspective of 'Body as Computation and Neuromorphic Control', reinforcement learning is shifting from simply optimizing controller parameters to co-designing body structures and neural mechanisms. The core idea of this direction is to utilize the dynamic characteristics of physical systems (such as muscle nonlinearity and passive stability) to share computational pressure, achieving minimal neural architectures. In terms of evolutionary path, early research relied mostly on traditional networks combined with complex physical simulations, while recent work (e.g., #2) proposes viewing the body as a sensor and physical parameters as weights, achieving complex motion with minimal neurons through the co-optimization of neuromorphic control and muscle networks. This marks a paradigm shift in embodied intelligence from 'brain-dominated' to 'body-mind fusion', aiming to solve high-dimensional control challenges and improve system robustness, although it still faces engineering challenges such as simulation-to-reality transfer and optimization efficiency.

2026-08
Co-design of Neural and Muscle Network based on Embodied Perceptron Representation
动机: Motivation: 传统强化学习依赖复杂神经网络处理感知与决策，计算成本高且缺乏生物启发的极简性，难以体现身体在智能中的计算作用。Traditional reinforcement learning relies on complex neural networks to process perception and decision-making, resulting in high computational costs and a lack of biologically inspired minimalism, making it difficult to reflect the computational role of the body in intelligence.
方法: Method: 提出基于具身感知器表示的神经与肌肉网络协同设计框架，将物理参数映射为权重、非线性动力学映射为激活函数，利用CMA-ES算法联合优化肌肉配置与单神经元控制器。Proposing a neural and muscle network co-design framework based on embodied sensor representations, mapping physical parameters to weights and nonlinear dynamics to activation functions, and utilizing the CMA-ES algorithm to jointly optimize muscle configurations and single-neuron controllers.
推进: Advance: 证明单神经元控制器配合特定肌肉配置即可实现行走，相比传统网络（需16个神经元）大幅降低计算复杂度，确立了身体结构作为计算载体的理论可行性。Demonstrating that a single-neuron controller combined with specific muscle configurations can achieve walking, significantly reducing computational complexity compared to traditional networks (requiring 16 neurons), and establishing the theoretical feasibility of body structure as a computational carrier.
2026-09
BRIDGE: An Open-Source Humanoid Platform via Morphology-Control Co-Design for Physical AI
动机: Motivation: 旨在打破机器人硬件设计与控制算法长期割裂的局面，探索通过形态与控制协同设计来释放物理系统的计算潜力。Aims to break the long-standing separation between robot hardware design and control algorithms, exploring the release of computational potential of physical systems through co-design of morphology and control.
方法: Method: 构建了BRIDGE开源人形平台，采用形态与控制联合优化的策略，利用旋转电机驱动的物理结构特性来辅助运动控制。Constructs the BRIDGE open-source humanoid platform, adopting a strategy of joint optimization of morphology and control, utilizing the physical structure characteristics driven by rotary motors to assist motion control.
推进: Advance: 证明了在极简硬件约束下，通过协同设计可实现超越主流竞品（如Bumi、K1）的运动保真度，将硬件设计正式纳入控制优化的闭环中。Proves that under minimal hardware constraints, co-design can achieve motion fidelity surpassing mainstream competitors (such as Bumi, K1), formally incorporating hardware design into the closed loop of control optimization.
2026-09
Seven Sources of Physical AI Capability Formation
动机: Motivation: 针对物理智能体能力来源模糊不清、难以区分数据驱动与进化驱动等形成路径的问题，亟需建立细粒度的分类框架以厘清“身体即计算”的演化机制。Addressing the ambiguity regarding the source of physical agent capabilities and the difficulty in distinguishing between data-driven and evolution-driven pathways, there is an urgent need to establish a fine-grained classification framework to clarify the evolution mechanism of 'body as computation'.
方法: Method: 将物理 AI 的能力形成来源拆解为七类，严格区分离线经验记录（RE）与在线反馈（EI）等不同范式，构建审计清单式的分类学体系。Deconstructed the sources of physical AI capability formation into seven categories, strictly distinguishing different paradigms such as offline experience recording (RE) and online interaction (EI), and constructed a taxonomy system in the form of an audit checklist.
推进: Advance: 首次将模糊的“数据驱动”概念解构为可量化的具体来源，为评估身体结构与神经机制协同进化的贡献提供了理论基准，尽管缺乏实证数据支撑。For the first time, deconstructed the vague concept of 'data-driven' into quantifiable specific sources, providing a theoretical benchmark for evaluating the contribution of the co-evolution of body structure and neural mechanisms, despite the lack of empirical data support.
